| 38 | 中文论文摘要信息 | dwd_zh_paper_abstract | 摘要 中文 | zh_abstract | longtext |  | 文献内容的中文摘要信息。 | 通常认为，一般人格权概念起源于德国，德国法将“人的尊严”及“人格自由发展”作为一般人格权的核心价值。我国《民法典》没有照搬这种说法，而是以概括的方式规定了基于人身自由及人格尊严而产生的一般人格权。一般人格权条款是一种框架性条款，而一般人格权是一种受民法保护的法益，包含“人格尊严”及“人身自由”两大核心价值。具体来说，人格尊严一方面指主体请求法律保护其民事权利能力的能力，另一方面指主体因其民事权利能力而受法律保护的人格权益抽象总和；人身自由指每个人在全部社会生活中一切与人格尊严实现有关系的所有人身自由。其中，人格尊严乃人身自由的渊源性价值，其为人身自由提供了正当化理由及方向指引。 | 是 | 否 | 否 | 否 |  |  |  | 90.81 |  |  |  |

| 38 | 英文论文摘要信息 | dwd_en_paper_abstract | 摘要 英文 | en_abstract | longtext |  | 文献内容的英文摘要信息。 | Natural soil and vegetation recovery following human disturbance is the primary means of restoring degraded ecosystems globally. However, it remains unclear how vegetation recovery in the tropical karst areas of China affects the soil physicochemical properties. Here, we investigated the impacts of natural vegetation recovery on soil physicochemical properties at different soil depths in tropical karst areas in southwestern China, using a space–time substitution method. We found that with the natural vegetation recovery, soil bulk density (SBD) decreased. Soil pH initially decreased and then increased, reaching its lowest value during the shrubland stage. There was a significant increase in other soil physicochemical factors. In the soil profiles, SBD tended to increase with depth. The pH, total potassium (TK), total phosphorus (TP), and exchangeable calcium remained relatively stable across the different soil layers. TK, TP, available phosphorus, SBD, total nitrogen, pH, exchangeable magnesium, and available potassium significantly contributed to the soil physicochemical properties. Soil physicochemical properties were predominantly directly affected by litter and biological factors, albeit indirectly influenced by topographic factors. Our study provides crucial insights into karst soils and their relationship with vegetation recovery, which are pivotal for steering vegetation restoration and soil amelioration in karst areas. | 是 | 否 | 否 | 否 |  |  |  | 63.27 |  |  |  |
| 39 | 英文论文摘要信息 | dwd_en_paper_abstract | 摘要 中文 | zh_abstract | longtext |  | 文献内容的中文摘要信息。 | 人为干扰后自然土壤与植被恢复是全球退化生态系统修复的主要途径。然而，中国热带喀斯特地区植被恢复如何影响土壤理化性质，目前仍不明确。本研究采用时空替代法，探究中国西南热带喀斯特地区自然植被恢复对不同土层深度土壤理化性质的影响。结果表明：随着自然植被恢复，土壤容重（SBD）呈下降趋势；土壤 pH 值先降后升，在灌丛阶段达到最低；其余土壤理化指标均显著提升。在土壤剖面中，土壤容重随土层深度增加呈上升趋势；pH 值、全钾（TK）、全磷（TP）和交换性钙在不同土层间保持相对稳定。全钾、全磷、有效磷、土壤容重、全氮、pH 值、交换性镁和速效钾是影响土壤理化性质的关键因子。土壤理化性质主要受枯落物与生物因子的直接影响，同时也受地形因子的间接调控。本研究结果可为喀斯特地区植被恢复与土壤改良提供重要理论依据。 | 是 | 否 | 否 | 否 |  |  |  | 63.26 |  |  |  |

| 114 | 专利摘要信息表 | dwd_patent_abstract | 英文摘要 | abstract_en | / | text |  | 专利英文摘要文本。 | {"en":"The invention discloses a paper handkerchief folding machining device. The paper handkerchief folding machining device comprises a longitudinal folding machine set, a transverse folding machine set and a flower plate machine set, wherein the longitudinal folding machine set, the transverse folding machine set and the flower plate machine set are sequentially jointed on a machine platform. Paper webs are sequentially arranged on the longitudinal folding machine set and the transverse folding machine set in a tensioning and penetrating mode. A paper handkerchief is folded into three folding surfaces in a Z-shaped mode in the width direction through a longitudinal folding machine, and refolded twice in the length direction to form four folding surfaces through a transverse folding machine. The paper handkerchief folding machining device is high in production efficiency and in speed and capable of reducing production cost. The size of produced paper can be adjusted, and products are convenient to carry and attractive."} | 是 | 否 | 否 | 否 |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
| 115 | 专利摘要信息表 | dwd_patent_abstract | 中文翻译摘要 | abstract_trans_cn | / | text |  | 专利摘要的中文翻译文本。 | 本发明公开了一种纸巾折叠加工装置。该纸巾折叠加工装置包括纵向折叠机组、横向折叠机组及花板机组，所述纵向折叠机组、横向折叠机组与花板机组依次衔接安装在机台上。纸幅以张紧穿设的方式依次布置于纵向折叠机组与横向折叠机组上。纸巾经纵向折叠机沿宽度方向 Z 形折叠形成三个折面，并通过横向折叠机沿长度方向二次折叠形成四个折面。本纸巾折面 **。本纸巾折叠加工装置生产效率高、速度快，可降低生产成本；所生产纸巾尺寸可调，产品便于携带且外形美观。 | 是 | 否 | 否 | 否 |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |

| 19 | 国内项目信息表 | dwd_zh_project | 项目标书摘要 | abstract |  | longtext |  | 项目申请书或标书中的摘要内容。 | 空天飞行器是世界大国争夺制空权和制天权的关键武器平台之一，近年来美国空天飞行器发展迅速，我国在空间飞行器平台研制中也取得重大进展，迫切需要装备并充分利用定向能、侦查遥感、激光通信等精密载荷以提升其战略应用潜力，然而空天飞行器突出的敏捷机动性和复杂的动力学特性极大限制了其在轨应用能力。本项目将针对敏捷机动空天飞行器的复杂动力学扰动问题，开展空天飞行器宽频多幅微振动的多尺度集成建模方法研究；设计主动减振界面隔离空天飞行器平台微振动，探索博弈鲁棒控制方法优化减振界面等效动刚度，保证隔振效率的同时兼顾平台敏捷机动性；针对敏感载荷端制冷扰动及目标相对运动，研究载荷指向宽频扰动补偿方法，探索基于在线辨识的自适应复合控制方法，抑制载荷光轴的不确定、时变扰动；最后将在前期研制设备的基础上开发实验系统验证本项目研究方法的有效性。本项目研究将为敏捷机动空天飞行器装备应用敏感精密载荷提供技术支持 | 是 | 否 | 否 | 否 |  |  |  | 69.1 |  |  |  |  |
| 20 | 国内项目信息表 | dwd_zh_project | 项目结题摘要 | final_report_abstract |  | longtext |  | 项目结题报告中的摘要内容。 | 空天飞行器是一种兼具航天和航空技术优势的飞行器，是世界各国争夺制空权和制天权的关键武器平台之一，配备先进载荷对于提升其任务能力和战略优势至关重要。然而，空天飞行器的敏捷性、机动性需求以及复杂的微振动环境与多种精密载荷的超静、超稳与超准要求形成矛盾，严重限制了载荷的可实现性能。本项目首先建立了空天飞行器微振动扰源与整器结构的多尺度集成动力学模型，研究了典型扰源微振动的产生和传递机理；在微振动建模和特性分析的基础上，针对空天飞行器设计了综合传递路径超静舱隔振和载荷端快反镜补偿的两级扰动抑制方案，并展开研究了敏捷机动情况下超静舱的宽频带微振动主动抑制和复杂时变扰动下载荷的超精超稳指向控制问题，提出了敏捷机动与隔振的博弈控制方法，实现超静舱动刚度优化以解决机动与隔振的矛盾，并针对复杂时变扰动给出了一种基于在线扰动估计和控制更新的自适应补偿方法；最后，设计并开发了多自由度超静舱和快反镜指向试验系统并完成了地面试验验证。本项目研究成果为敏捷机动空天飞行器精密载荷的高性能应用提供了技术支撑。基于以上研究内容，共完成发表期刊论文8篇，录用期刊论文1篇，其中，该项目负责人作为第一/通信作者的期刊论文7篇，申请发明专利共3件 | 是 | 否 | 否 | 否 |  |  |  | 47.03 | 1. 2014 年才启动全文公开，2014 年前结题项目无公开摘要。2. 基金委对近 10 年 + 无涉密 / 敏感 / 商业秘密的项目才开放；涉密、涉及核心技术或未脱敏项目不予公开 |  |  |  |

| 20 | 国外项目信息表 | dwd_en_project | 项目标书摘要 | abstract |  | longtext |  | 项目申请书或标书中的摘要内容。 | The University of Delaware(UD)proposes to support oceanographic technical services on R/V Hugh R.Sharp operated as part of the U.S.Academic Research Fleet(ARF),which is scheduled by the University-National Oceanographic Laboratory System(UNOLS).As part of their basic operations,UD will provide shipboard technicians on each seagoing research project to support basic services.Technicians will maintain,calibrate and provide for qualified users,items from their pool of shared-use research instrumentation.Research vessels in the ARF provide support for researchers from a variety of federal and state agencies,as well as some private sponsors.All users(or the appropriate funding agencies)share support costs for basic technical services on the vessel equally,via a day-rate,with each paying a share of the costs based on fractional usage of the vessel.The principal impact of the present proposal is under Merit Review Criterion 2 of the Proposal Guidelines(NSF 23-525).It provides infrastructure support for scientists to use the vessel and its shared-use instrumentation in support of their NSF-funded oceanographic research projects(which individually undergo separate review by the relevant research program of NSF).The acquisition,maintenance and operation of shared-use instrumentation allows NSF-funded researchers from any US university or lab access to working,calibrated instruments for their research,reducing the cost of that research,and expanding the base of potential researchers.This award reflects NSF's statutory mission and has been deemed worthy of support through evaluation using the Foundation's intellectual merit and broader impacts review criteria. | 是 | 否 | 否 | 否 |  |  |  | 96.47 |  |  |  |  |
| 21 | 国外项目信息表 | dwd_en_project | 项目结题摘要 | final_report_abstract |  | longtext |  | 项目结题报告中的摘要内容。 | The University of Delaware(UD)proposes to support oceanographic technical services on R/V Hugh R.Sharp operated as part of the U.S.Academic Research Fleet(ARF),which is scheduled by the University-National Oceanographic Laboratory System(UNOLS).As part of their basic operations,UD will provide shipboard technicians on each seagoing research project to support basic services.Technicians will maintain,calibrate and provide for qualified users,items from their pool of shared-use research instrumentation.Research vessels in the ARF provide support for researchers from a variety of federal and state agencies,as well as some private sponsors.All users(or the appropriate funding agencies)share support costs for basic technical services on the vessel equally,via a day-rate,with each paying a share of the costs based on fractional usage of the vessel.The principal impact of the present proposal is under Merit Review Criterion 2 of the Proposal Guidelines(NSF 23-525).It provides infrastructure support for scientists to use the vessel and its shared-use instrumentation in support of their NSF-funded oceanographic research projects(which individually undergo separate review by the relevant research program of NSF).The acquisition,maintenance and operation of shared-use instrumentation allows NSF-funded researchers from any US university or lab access to working,calibrated instruments for their research,reducing the cost of that research,and expanding the base of potential researchers.This award reflects NSF's statutory mission and has been deemed worthy of support through evaluation using the Foundation's intellectual merit and broader impacts review criteria. | 是 | 否 | 否 | 否 |  |  |  | 76.3 |  |  |  |  |

| 6 | 中文科技报告信息表 | dwd_zh_report | 中文摘要 | abstract_cn | longtext |  | 报告内容的简介信息。 | 家族肥厚型心肌病（FHCM）是一种最常见的遗传性心血管疾病，2014年欧洲心脏学会（ESC）指南公布FHCM一级亲属确诊标准是左室节段厚度大于等于13mm，文献和我们以往的实际临床工作发现厚度未及此标准的部分一级亲属超声心动图已出现舒张功能减低和心电图异常，然而目前除建议每年体检随访外，对于这部分家庭成员早期症状的诊断以及是否采取干预尚无明确定论。本项目拟通过结合多种影像诊断方法（超声心动图、心脏核磁和心电图）系统测量和评价心脏结构、血流以及整体和节段收缩和舒张功能、纤维化以及心脏电活动的改变，应用数据融合技术建立基因型和影像表型的关系，找出FHCM典型突变MYH7携带者无损影像检测的特征，判断FHCM患者的一级亲属是否MYH7突变基因的携带者以及是否开始发病。这些研究成果能对FHCM成员进行早期诊断，进行合理预防和指导个体化治疗，既合理地利用医疗资源，又减少了不必要的干预和并发症。 | 是 | 否 | 否 | 否 |  |  |  | 0.9999 |  |  |  |  |
